```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/user_0a329be7/code/clash-verge-ai-routing/README.md",
  "content": "# clash-verge-ai-routing\n\nPersonal Clash Verge Rev profile extensions that route all AI traffic (Anthropic, OpenAI/ChatGPT, xAI/Grok, Google Gemini, DeepSeek) through a chained proxy: `trojan(JP/HK) → kookeey ISP Proxy (LA, [REDACTED])`, designed for use behind GFW with home broadband.\n\n**Why this exists:**\n1. AI providers reject traffic from Datacenter / VPN ASNs (HK/JP airport proxies → 403/cf-challenge / abuse-detection).\n2. WebShare's regular *residential* tier blocks AI hostnames at CONNECT-time on a per-source-IP basis.\n3. kookeey's *ISP Proxy* tier has no hostname blocklist, but its TCP endpoints are unreachable from CN home broadband (firewall drops SYN).\n4. Solution: chain a trojan exit (JP/HK) → kookeey LA. mihomo's `dialer-proxy` field implements this natively. CF anycast no longer routes by client region after chaining — paths converge at LA → AI backend.\n\n## Files\n\nAll five files preserve their original UIDs (Clash Verge identifies extensions by filename). Do **not** rename.\n\n| UID filename | Role | What it does |\n|---|---|---|\n| `profiles/pio6Fbt0EFs7.yaml` | **proxies** | Defines two `socks5` proxies (`kookeey-via-HK01`, `kookeey-via-JP03`) with `dialer-proxy` pointing at named trojan nodes in the WestData subscription. |\n| `profiles/gnj7wCDWoG9N.yaml` | **groups** | Defines `AI` group as `url-test` over the two kookeey chains, 300s health-check interval, 50ms tolerance. Auto-failover. |\n| `profiles/r7Ve1TfXTY9F.yaml` | **rules** | Prepends AI-domain routing rules (anthropic / claude.ai / claude.com / openai / chatgpt / oai* / sora / arkose / livekit / xai / grok / generativelanguage / aistudio / deepseek + Anthropic-own telemetry endpoints). |\n| `profiles/m7TlnBeJeJWD.yaml` | **merge** | Sets `profile.store-selected: true`. |\n| `profiles/sIJUEQAp2rlw.js` | **script** | No-op placeholder (returns config unchanged). |\n\n## Restore on a new machine (macOS)\n\n```bash\n# 1. Install Clash Verge Rev and add your WestData / equivalent subscription via the GUI.\n#    Note the new subscription's UID (e.g. RHK9R9qqTbTO) — Verge generates a fresh one per machine.\n\n# 2. Clone this repo and copy the extension files (preserving UID filenames):\ngit clone git@github.com:wzyfromhust/clash-verge-ai-routing.git\ncd clash-verge-ai-routing\n./restore.sh   # macOS only; copies files + reminds you what to do in the GUI\n\n# 3. In Clash Verge GUI:\n#    - Open Profiles tab\n#    - Each extension UID file will appear automatically as an unattached extension.\n#    - Click your subscription profile → \"Edit\" → attach all 5 extensions\n#      (Merge, Script, Rules, Proxies, Groups) using the dropdowns.\n#    - Click \"Apply\".\n\n# 4. Verify (after Verge re-merges into clash-verge.yaml):\ncurl -s --unix-socket /tmp/verge/verge-mihomo.sock http://localhost/proxies/AI \\\n  | python3 -c \"import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['type'], d['now'], d['all'])\"\n# expect: URLTest kookeey-via-HK01 ['kookeey-via-HK01', 'kookeey-via-JP03']\n```\n\n**Other platforms:**\n- Linux: `~/.config/io.github.clash-verge-rev.clash-verge-rev/profiles/`\n- Windows: `%APPDATA%\\io.github.clash-verge-rev.clash-verge-rev\\profiles\\`\n\n## Required upstream nodes\n\nThe `dialer-proxy` fields in `pio6Fbt0EFs7.yaml` reference these node names — they must exist in your subscription:\n- `🇭🇰 Hong Kong | 01`\n- `🇯🇵 Japan | 03`\n\nIf your subscription names nodes differently, edit `pio6Fbt0EFs7.yaml` accordingly. The two were chosen after empirical chained-latency testing across all JP01-08 / HK01-08 candidates (HK01 had the lowest variance + lowest avg; JP03 was the closest second for failover).\n\n## Sensitive data\n\nThis repo **must stay private**. It contains:\n- kookeey SOCKS5 credentials (`[REDACTED]:[REDACTED]:24754`).\n\nRotating credentials: edit `pio6Fbt0EFs7.yaml`, commit, push, re-attach in Verge GUI.\n\n## What is NOT in this repo (intentionally)\n\n- The WestData subscription URL / token — pull it from your subscription provider on each machine.\n- `profiles.yaml` — Verge regenerates this with new UIDs per-machine; the extension files attach via the GUI.\n- `clash-verge.yaml` — generated output; rebuilt from extensions + subscription on each apply.\n\n## Routing map\n\n```\n本机 → Clash Verge (mixed-port 7897 + TUN)\n        ├─ AI domain match → AI group (url-test)\n        │   ├─ kookeey-via-HK01 (socks5)\n        │   │   └─ dialer-proxy: 🇭🇰 Hong Kong | 01 (trojan)\n        │   │       └─ us17.kookeey.info:24754 → [REDACTED] (LA) → AI backend\n        │   └─ kookeey-via-JP03 (socks5)\n        │       └─ dialer-proxy: 🇯🇵 Japan | 03 (trojan)\n        │           └─ us17.kookeey.info:24754 → [REDACTED] (LA) → AI backend\n        └─ everything else → Proxies group (regular trojan, JP)\n```\n\n## AI domains routed through kookeey\n\n**Anthropic** (api / a-api / a-cdn / api-staging / console / docs / mcp-proxy / status / support / www):\n- `anthropic.com` (suffix)\n- `claude.ai` (suffix, covers assets/downloads/pivot/preview/a.claude.ai)\n- `claude.com` (suffix, covers code/docs/mcp/platform/preview/privacy/support)\n- `anthropic-cdn.com` (suffix, legacy)\n- `ingest.us.sentry.io` (suffix, Anthropic's Sentry project o1158394)\n- `browser-intake-us5-datadoghq.com`, `http-intake.logs.us5.datadoghq.com` (Anthropic client telemetry)\n\n**OpenAI / ChatGPT**:\n- `openai.com` / `chatgpt.com` / `oaiusercontent.com` / `oaistatic.com` / `sora.com` (suffix)\n- Azure CDN: `openaiapi-site.azureedge.net`, `openaicom.imgix.net` (suffix); `openaicom-api-bdcpf8c6d2e9atf6.z01.azurefd.net`, `openaicomproductionae4b.blob.core.windows.net`, `production-openaicom-storage.azureedge.net`, `chat.openai.com.cdn.cloudflare.net` (exact)\n- Arkose CAPTCHA: `openai-api.arkoselabs.com` (exact), `client-api.arkoselabs.com` (suffix)\n- LiveKit voice: `chatgpt.livekit.cloud`, `host.livekit.cloud`, `turn.livekit.cloud` (suffix)\n- Keyword `openai` (catch-all for variants)\n\n**xAI / Grok**:\n- `x.ai` (suffix, covers api/console/accounts)\n- `grok.com` (suffix)\n- `xai-cdn.com` (suffix, preemptive)\n\n**Google Gemini**:\n- `generativelanguage.googleapis.com` (suffix)\n- `aistudio.google.com` (suffix)\n\n**DeepSeek**:\n- `deepseek.com` (suffix)\n\n## Intentionally NOT routed through kookeey (let them go via default Proxies group)\n\n- Generic SaaS used by many apps including ChatGPT: `auth0.com`, `stripe.com`, `intercom.io`, `segment.io`, `statsig.com`, `launchdarkly.com`, `featuregates.org`, `algolia.net`, `cloudflareinsights.com` — too broad, would waste kookeey bandwidth.\n- Apple Intelligence ChatGPT integration: `smoot.apple.com`, `guzzoni.apple.com`, `gateway.icloud.com` — subscription's OpenAI group handles these; Apple's request comes from the Apple cloud relay anyway, not the user.\n- GitHub / npm / Google APIs (non-Gemini) — claude-code's MCP and tool integrations work fine through the regular Proxies group.\n"
```

## 仓库结构

```
clash-verge-ai-routing/
├── README.md            完整说明 + 设计 rationale + 域名清单
├── restore.sh           macOS 一键恢复脚本（半自动）
├── .gitignore
└── profiles/            5 个 extension（保留原 UID 文件名，Verge 必需）
    ├── pio6Fbt0EFs7.yaml   proxies: kookeey chains
    ├── gnj7wCDWoG9N.yaml   groups: AI url-test
    ├── r7Ve1TfXTY9F.yaml   rules: AI domain routing
    ├── m7TlnBeJeJWD.yaml   merge: store-selected
    └── sIJUEQAp2rlw.js     script: no-op
```

## 换电脑恢复流程（README 里有完整版）

```bash
# 新机器：安装 Clash Verge Rev → 添加 WestData 订阅 → 退出 Verge
git clone git@github.com:wzyfromhust/clash-verge-ai-routing.git
cd clash-verge-ai-routing
./restore.sh   # 自动 copy 5 个 extension 到 ~/Library/Application Support/io.github.clash-verge-rev.clash-verge-rev/profiles/

# 开 Verge → Profiles → 当前订阅 → Edit → 把 5 个 extension 通过下拉绑上 → Apply
```

## 几个关键约束（README 里已写明）

1. **保留 UID 文件名**：Verge 用 UID 关联 extension，重命名会断关联
2. **`dialer-proxy` 引用的节点名必须存在**：`🇭🇰 Hong Kong | 01` / `🇯🇵 Japan | 03` —— 不同订阅源节点命名不同，需要按需改 `pio6Fbt0EFs7.yaml`
3. **kookeey 凭据在仓库里** —— 这就是必须 private 的原因；旋转凭据时改文件 → 重 push → restore.sh 再跑一次

## 故意没放进去的（避免泄漏）

- WestData 订阅 URL/token（每台机器重新拿）
- `profiles.yaml`（含订阅元数据，每机器自动重生成）
- `clash-verge.yaml`（generated output，apply 时自动重建）

如果你之后想加更多机器同步、改 kookeey 凭据、加新 AI 厂商，直接 `git add → commit → push`，新机器 `git pull → ./restore.sh` 就好。

> AGENT

数据出来了，整理报告：

## kookeey 上行质量（当前主选 HK01 链路）

| 测试 | 数据 | 解读 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 MB 单流上行 | **8.5 Mbps** / 0.98s | RTT 主导 + TLS 握手开销 |
| **5 MB 单流上行（峰值）** | **18.7 Mbps** / 2.24s | TCP cwnd 稳定后实际带宽 |
| 10 MB 单流上行 | 14.0 Mbps / 6.01s | 长流被 trojan/kookeey QoS 软限速 |
| 4 并发 × 5 MB | 单流 ~4.8 Mbps，**总 16.2 Mbps** | 公平均分，无单流 starvation；总量接近单流峰值，说明 18 Mbps 是链路天花板 |

## 上下行对比（同一条 HK01 链路）

| 方向 | 单流峰值 | 4 并发总 |
|---|---|---|
| **下行** | 20.7 Mbps | 25.1 Mbps |
| **上行** | 18.7 Mbps | 16.2 Mbps |

**上下行基本对称（90%）**，这其实意外地好：国内家宽通常上行只有下行的 1/4～1/2，但加了海外代理后瓶颈不在你家宽，而在 trojan 节点出口 + kookeey 落地的最窄段，正好对称。

## 对 AI 用例的实际意义

| 场景 | 上传量级 | 在 18 Mbps 下耗时 |
|---|---|---|
| 普通 API 请求（system + 几轮对话） | 30-200 KB | 13-90 ms |
| 大 context（满载 200K token 文本） | ~600 KB | ~270 ms |
| Claude Code 单次工具调用回传 | 50 KB - 1 MB | 22-440 ms |
| 极端：上传一个 5 MB 文件附件 | 5 MB | 2.2 s |
| 一次 1M token Opus 上下文 | ~3-4 MB | ~1.7 s |

**结论：完全够用**。对 Claude API / claude-code 的实际使用体验，上行从不是瓶颈 —— RTT (链式 ~1s 握手 + LA→AI ~30ms) 才是主导。

## 备注

- **JP03 链路未单测**（需切换 group selection，会影响你正在使用的连接，违背"不许切换"约束）。但握手延迟实测 JP03 与 HK01 差 5-15ms 在噪音内，HK→LA 海缆与 JP→LA 海缆容量级别相同，带宽预计在 ±10% 内。
- **10 MB 单流降速到 14 Mbps**：长流被中间某段（很可能是 HK01 trojan 节点）的 QoS 软限速。实际 AI 用例几乎从不会有连续 10 MB+ 的上行流，可忽略。
- **kookeey 自身没限上行配额**（套餐里只看流量总额 200 GB，不区分方向）。

```
=== /usr/bin/mihomo-update-config（订阅拉取脚本）===
#!/bin/sh
# Fetch subscription, merge with overrides, write /etc/mihomo/config.yaml
set -eu

SUB_URL="https://dy.boost1.shop/d/1def4f7198db6cc1dcabc4e70b8e0d0d"
DIR="/etc/mihomo"
SUB="$DIR/sub.yaml"
OUT="$DIR/config.yaml"
TMP="$(mktemp)"
trap "rm -f $TMP" EXIT

if ! curl -fL --retry 3 --max-time 30 -A "clash.meta" -o "$SUB.new" "$SUB_URL"; then
    echo "subscription download failed; keeping existing $SUB" >&2
    [ -f "$SUB" ] || exit 1
else
    if ! grep -q "^proxies:" "$SUB.new" || ! grep -q "^rules:" "$SUB.new"; then
        echo "subscription content invalid; keeping existing $SUB" >&2
        rm -f "$SUB.new"
        [ -f "$SUB" ] || exit 1
    else
        mv "$SUB.new" "$SUB"
    fi
fi

{
cat <<'PREAMBLE'
# AUTO-GENERATED by /usr/bin/mihomo-update-config — do not edit by hand.
mixed-port: 7890
redir-port: 7892
tproxy-port: 7893
allow-lan: true
bind-address: "*"
mode: rule
log-level: info
ipv6: false
external-controller: 127.0.0.1:9090
unified-delay: true
tcp-concurrent: true
geodata-mode: false
geo-auto-update: true
geo-update-interval: 168
geox-url:
  mmdb: "https://github.com/MetaCubeX/meta-rules-dat/releases/download/latest/geoip.metadb"

sniffer:
  enable: true
  force-dns-mapping: true
  parse-pure-ip: true
  sniff:
    TLS:
      ports: [443, 8443]
    HTTP:
      ports: [80, 8080-8880]
      override-destination: false
    QUIC:
      ports: [443]

dns:
  enable: true
  listen: 127.0.0.1:1053
  ipv6: false
  enhanced-mode: redir-host
  default-nameserver:
    - [REDACTED]
    - [REDACTED]
  nameserver:
    - https://doh.pub/dns-query
    - https://dns.alidns.com/dns-query
  proxy-server-nameserver:
    - https://doh.pub/dns-query

rule-providers:
  cn-app-ads:
    type: file
    behavior: domain
    format: text
    path: ./ruleset/cn-app-ads.txt
  anti-ad:
    type: http
    behavior: domain
    format: text
    url: "https://fastly.jsdelivr.net/gh/privacy-protection-tools/anti-AD@master/anti-ad-domains.txt"
    path: ./ruleset/anti-ad.txt
    interval: 86400
  awavenue:
    type: http
    behavior: domain
    format: yaml
    url: "https://fastly.jsdelivr.net/gh/TG-Twilight/AWAvenue-Ads-Rule@main/Filters/AWAvenue-Ads-Rule-Clash.yaml"
    path: ./ruleset/awavenue.yaml
    interval: 86400

PREAMBLE

awk '
BEGIN { state="skip" }
/^[a-zA-Z_-]+:/ {
    if ($0 ~ /^proxies:/)            { state="proxies"; print; print "    - { name: \"家宽-Anthropic\", type: http, server: [REDACTED], port: 6539, username: obodkrzx, [REDACTED], udp: false, tls: false }"; next }
    else if ($0 ~ /^proxy-groups:/)  { state="groups"; print; next }
```

```
/^[a-zA-Z_-]+:/ {

=== /etc/init.d/iphone-mihomo ===
#!/bin/sh /etc/rc.common

START=99
STOP=10
USE_PROCD=1

PHONE_MAC="ce:e9:f0:68:71:79"
REDIR_PORT="7892"
TABLE="iphone_mihomo"

setup_nft() {
    nft delete table inet "$TABLE" 2>/dev/null || true
    nft -f - <<EONFT
table inet $TABLE {
    chain prerouting {
        type nat hook prerouting priority dstnat - 5; policy accept;
        # Allow LAN-internal traffic to pass through unchanged
        ether saddr $PHONE_MAC ip daddr 10.0.0.0/8 counter return comment "lan direct"
        ether saddr $PHONE_MAC ip daddr 192.168.0.0/16 counter return comment "lan direct"
        ether saddr $PHONE_MAC ip daddr 172.16.0.0/12 counter return comment "lan direct"
        # Redirect all other TCP from iPhone to mihomo redir-port
        ether saddr $PHONE_MAC meta l4proto tcp counter redirect to :$REDIR_PORT comment "iphone -> mihomo"
        # Block QUIC (UDP/443) from iPhone to force HTTPS over TCP
        ether saddr $PHONE_MAC udp dport 443 counter reject with icmp port-unreachable comment "force https over tcp"
        # Reject all IPv6 from iPhone (force v4)
        ether saddr $PHONE_MAC meta nfproto ipv6 counter reject with icmpv6 port-unreachable comment "force ipv4"
    }
}
EONFT
}

start_service() {
    setup_nft
}

stop_service() {
    nft delete table inet "$TABLE" 2>/dev/null || true
}

reload_service() {
    setup_nft
}
```

完全理解了。架构总结：

| 组件 | 行为 |
|---|---|
| `/etc/init.d/iphone-mihomo` | **nftables 规则**：按 MAC `ce:e9:f0:68:71:79` 把 iPhone TCP 透明 redirect 到主 mihomo 7892，阻 QUIC、阻 IPv6 |
| `/etc/init.d/mihomo` | 单个 mihomo 进程，跑 `/etc/mihomo/config.yaml` |
| `/usr/bin/mihomo-update-config` (cron 12h) | 拉订阅 → awk merge 注入「家宽-Anthropic webshare residential」proxy + 4 条 anthropic 规则 |
| iPhone 网关 | 10.0.0.1，只它一个 MAC 走代理；其他设备不走 |

> DEVELOPER

全局实例？ 也只对iphone生效么？

> AGENT

对，澄清一下我之前说得不准确的地方：

**mihomo 进程是全局单实例**（一个 `/usr/bin/mihomo` PID 9106，listen 在 7890/7892/7893）。`iphone-mihomo` 这个 init script **不启动 mihomo 进程**，它只是一段 nftables 规则。

**生效范围由 nft 规则的 MAC 匹配决定 ——只有 iPhone 那个 MAC `ce:e9:f0:68:71:79` 的流量被 redirect 到 mihomo redir-port。** 其它设备（你这台 Mac、家里其它手机/电脑）的包在路由器 prerouting 阶段不匹配这条规则，按普通 NAT 直出，**完全不进 mihomo**。

```
iPhone (MAC ce:e9:f0:68:71:79) → 路由器 PREROUTING 命中 nft → DNAT to 127.0.0.1:7892 (mihomo redir) → mihomo 走 config.yaml 规则 → 出墙
其它设备 → 路由器 PREROUTING 没命中 → 普通 NAT → 直连出墙（被 GFW 拦/不拦看域名）
```

所以"全局 mihomo 实例 + 只 iPhone 走代理"是当前路由器的真实形态。改 mihomo 配置只影响 iPhone（以及任何后续被 nft 规则匹配的 MAC）。

要继续把 mihomo 配置切到 WestData 订阅 + 加 kookeey 链式代理 + 完整 AI 分流吗？还是想先调整 nft 规则的覆盖范围（比如让某些其它设备也走 mihomo）？

```json
  "description": "Capture live chain info for AI hosts"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "python3 <<'PY'\n\"\"\"保持连接 8 秒，期间抓 router mihomo connections\"\"\"\nimport socket, ssl, threading, time, json\nimport urllib.request, urllib.parse\nHOST = \"claude.ai\"\n\ndef hold_connection():\n    s = socket.socket(); s.settimeout(15)\n    s.connect((\"10.0.0.1\", 7890))\n    s.sendall(f\"CONNECT {HOST}:443 HTTP/1.1\\r\\nHost: {HOST}:443\\r\\n\\r\\n\".encode())\n    r=b\"\"\n    while b\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\" not in r:\n        c=s.recv(4096)\n        if not c: break\n        r+=c\n    ctx=ssl.create_default_context()\n    ss=ctx.wrap_socket(s,server_hostname=HOST)\n    ss.sendall(f\"GET /cdn-cgi/trace HTTP/1.1\\r\\nHost: {HOST}\\r\\nConnection: keep-alive\\r\\n\\r\\n\".encode())\n    end = time.time() + 8\n    while time.time() < end:\n        try:\n            c = ss.recv(4096)\n            if not c: break\n        except: break\n    ss.close()\n\nth = threading.Thread(target=hold_connection); th.start()\ntime.sleep(2)\nimport subprocess\nresult = subprocess.run([\"ssh\",\"root@10.0.0.1\",\"curl -s http://127.0.0.1:9090/connections\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nth.join(timeout=10)\nd = json.loads(result.stdout)\nai_hits = 0\nall_relevant = []\nfor c in d.get(\"connections\", []):\n    m = c.get(\"metadata\", {})\n    h = m.get(\"host\", \"\") or m.get(\"sniffHost\",\"\")\n    ch = c.get(\"chains\", [])\n    rule = c.get(\"rule\",\"\") + \"/\" + (c.get(\"rulePayload\") or \"\")\n    if any(k in h.lower() for k in [\"anthropic\",\"claude\",\"openai\",\"chatgpt\",\"x.ai\",\"grok\",\"deepseek\"]):\n        in_ai = ch and ch[-1]==\"AI\"\n        if in_ai: ai_hits += 1\n        marker = \"✓\" if in_ai else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  {marker} {h:45s}  rule={rule:30s}  chain={' → '.join(ch)}\")\n        all_relevant.append(h)\nprint(f\"\\n命中 AI: {ai_hits} / 共 {len(all_relevant)} 个 AI 相关连接\")\nPY",
  "description": "Hold connection while inspecting"
}
```

iPhone 流量配置完整改造完成。总结：

## 路由器现状

| 项 | 状态 |
|---|---|
| 订阅 | BoostNet → **WestData**（静态 sub.yaml，不再依赖远程 URL） |
| mihomo 实例 | 全局单实例，listen 7890/7892/7893/9090 |
| 生效范围 | **仅 iPhone MAC `ce:e9:f0:68:71:79`**（10.0.0.153）通过 nft redirect 进 mihomo |
| 旧 webshare 代理 | ✓ 删除（被 hostname 黑名单的 `[REDACTED]:6539`） |
| AI url-test group | ✓ 加载，主选 `kookeey-via-HK01`（fallback `kookeey-via-JP03`） |
| AI 规则数 | 33 条（anthropic / claude / openai / chatgpt / oai* / sora / arkose / livekit / xai / grok / gemini / deepseek + Anthropic 自家 sentry/datadog 遥测）|
| 实测出口 IP | **[REDACTED] LA (colo=LAX)** ✓ |
| AI 域名命中率 | 7/7 (anthropic/claude/chatgpt/openai/xai/gemini/deepseek) 全通 |
| iPhone QUIC/IPv6 | 仍被 nft 阻断（保持原策略） |

## 关键改动文件

1. `/etc/mihomo/sub.yaml` —— 静态 WestData 订阅（从 Mac scp 过来，196KB / 4575 行）
2. `/usr/bin/mihomo-update-config` —— 重写，注入 kookeey 双链 + AI group + 完整规则
3. `/etc/mihomo/config.yaml` —— 自动生成（4665 行，重启已生效）

## 备份在路由器上

- `/etc/mihomo/sub.yaml.boostnet-bak`（旧订阅）
- `/etc/mihomo/config.yaml.bak-1779807343`（旧 config）
- `/usr/bin/mihomo-update-config.boostnet-bak`（旧脚本）

回滚一条命令：`mv /usr/bin/mihomo-update-config.boostnet-bak /usr/bin/mihomo-update-config && /usr/bin/mihomo-update-config && /etc/init.d/mihomo restart`

## cron 行为

`17 */12 * * * /usr/bin/mihomo-update-config && /etc/init.d/mihomo restart` 保持不变。新脚本不拉远程 URL，所以这只是每 12h restart 一次 mihomo（idempotent），iPhone 断 1-2s 然后无感恢复。如果你想去掉这个无意义的周期 restart，告诉我。

iPhone 上现在打开 Claude.app / ChatGPT / Grok 网页都应该直接走 LA 出口，跟你 Mac 上一样。

> AGENT

**关键发现：**
1. soar 是 Docker 容器（172.17.0.5 docker bridge），但出口走宿主机 **[REDACTED] 美国 ColoCrossing**
2. **kookeey 24754 直连可达 ✓**（美国境内，无 IP ACL 阻挡）
3. WestData trojan 节点不通（trojan server 拒绝美国来源）

soar 不需要 chain，**直接 SOCKS5 连 kookeey** 即可：少一段中转，更快更稳。修脚本：

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
```json
{
  "command": "cat > /tmp/soar-mihomo-update-config <<'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\n# Build /etc/mihomo/config.yaml from the static WestData subscription\n# + soar-local preamble + injected kookeey-direct + AI routing rules.\n#\n# soar runs in a container with US datacenter egress ([REDACTED]).\n# Direct SOCKS5 to kookeey works — no chained trojan needed.\n# WestData trojan nodes are not reachable from US-egress (CN-source-only).\nset -euo pipefail\n\nDIR=/etc/mihomo\nSUB=$DIR/providers/WestData.yaml\nOUT=$DIR/config.yaml\nTMP=$(mktemp)\ntrap \"rm -f $TMP\" EXIT\n\nif [[ ! -f \"$SUB\" ]]; then\n    echo \"missing $SUB; place static WestData subscription cache first\" >&2\n    exit 1\nfi\n\n{\ncat <<'PREAMBLE'\n# AUTO-GENERATED by /usr/local/bin/mihomo-update-config — do not edit by hand.\nmixed-port: 7890\nsocks-port: 7891\nallow-lan: true\nmode: rule\nlog-level: info\nipv6: false\n\nexternal-controller: 0.0.0.0:9090\nsecret: \"\"\n\ndns:\n  enable: true\n  ipv6: false\n  enhanced-mode: fake-ip\n  fake-ip-range: [REDACTED]/16\n  fake-ip-filter:\n    - \"*.lan\"\n    - localhost.ptlogin2.qq.com\n  nameserver:\n    - 8.8.8.8\n    - 1.1.1.1\n\nPREAMBLE\n\nawk '\nBEGIN { state=\"skip\" }\n/^[a-zA-Z_-]+:/ {\n    if ($0 ~ /^proxies:/) {\n        state=\"proxies\"; print\n        # kookeey ISP Proxy — direct SOCKS5 (soar already has US egress)\n        print \"  - {name: \\\"kookeey-direct\\\", type: socks5, server: us17.kookeey.info, port: 24754, username: \\\"[REDACTED]\\\", password: \\\"[REDACTED]\\\", udp: false}\"\n        next\n    }\n    else if ($0 ~ /^proxy-groups:/) {\n        state=\"groups\"; print\n        # AI group → kookeey (so all soar AI traffic exits [REDACTED] LA, same as Mac/iPhone)\n        print \"  - {name: AI, type: select, proxies: [kookeey-direct, DIRECT]}\"\n        next\n    }\n    else if ($0 ~ /^rules:/) {\n        state=\"rules\"; print\n        # === Anthropic + own telemetry ===\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,anthropic.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,claude.ai,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,claude.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,anthropic-cdn.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,claudeusercontent.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,anthropicusercontent.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,ingest.us.sentry.io,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,browser-intake-us5-datadoghq.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,http-intake.logs.us5.datadoghq.com,AI\"\n        # === OpenAI / ChatGPT ===\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,openai.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,chatgpt.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,oaiusercontent.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,oaistatic.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,sora.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,chat.openai.com.cdn.cloudflare.net,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,openaicom-api-bdcpf8c6d2e9atf6.z01.azurefd.net,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,openaicomproductionae4b.blob.core.windows.net,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,production-openaicom-storage.azureedge.net,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,openaiapi-site.azureedge.net,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,openaicom.imgix.net,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN,openai-api.arkoselabs.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,client-api.arkoselabs.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,chatgpt.livekit.cloud,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,host.livekit.cloud,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,turn.livekit.cloud,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-KEYWORD,openai,AI\"\n        # === xAI / Grok ===\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,x.ai,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,grok.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,xai-cdn.com,AI\"\n        # === Google Gemini ===\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,generativelanguage.googleapis.com,AI\"\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,aistudio.google.com,AI\"\n        # === DeepSeek ===\n        print \" - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,deepseek.com,AI\"\n        next\n    }\n    else { state=\"skip\"; next }\n}\n{ if (state != \"skip\") print }\n' \"$SUB\"\n} > \"$TMP\"\n\nif ! /usr/local/bin/mihomo -d \"$DIR\" -f \"$TMP\" -t >/tmp/mihomo-test.log 2>&1; then\n    echo \"mihomo config validation FAILED:\" >&2\n    cat /tmp/mihomo-test.log >&2\n    exit 1\nfi\n\nmv \"$TMP\" \"$OUT\"\necho \"wrote $OUT ($(wc -l < \"$OUT\") lines)\"\nSCRIPT\n\nscp -q /tmp/soar-mihomo-update-config soar:/usr/local/bin/mihomo-update-config\nssh soar 'chmod +x /usr/local/bin/mihomo-update-config && /usr/local/bin/mihomo-update-config' 2>&1",
```

```json
  "command": "sleep 4\nssh soar 'echo \"=== AI 域名命中测试 + chain ===\"\npython3 <<\"PY\"\nimport socket, ssl, time, threading, json, urllib.request, urllib.parse\nHOSTS = [\"api.anthropic.com\",\"claude.ai\",\"chatgpt.com\",\"api.openai.com\",\"api.x.ai\",\"grok.com\",\"generativelanguage.googleapis.com\",\"api.deepseek.com\",\"sora.com\"]\ndef fetch(host):\n    try:\n        s=socket.socket(); s.settimeout(15); s.connect((\"127.0.0.1\",7890))\n        s.sendall(f\"CONNECT {host}:443 HTTP/1.1\\r\\nHost: {host}:443\\r\\n\\r\\n\".encode())\n        r=b\"\"\n        while b\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\" not in r:\n            c=s.recv(4096)\n            if not c: break\n            r+=c\n        ctx=ssl.create_default_context()\n        ss=ctx.wrap_socket(s,server_hostname=host)\n        ss.sendall(f\"HEAD / HTTP/1.1\\r\\nHost: {host}\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n\".encode())\n        end=time.time()+5\n        while time.time()<end:\n            try:\n                c=ss.recv(4096)\n                if not c: break\n            except: break\n        ss.close()\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f\"err: {e}\"\n    return \"ok\"\n\nfor h in HOSTS:\n    th=threading.Thread(target=fetch, args=(h,)); th.start()\n\ntime.sleep(1.8)\ndata=json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(\"http://127.0.0.1:9090/connections\", timeout=3).read())\nfor c in (data.get(\"connections\") or []):\n    h=c.get(\"metadata\",{}).get(\"host\",\"\")\n    if h in HOSTS:\n        ch=c.get(\"chains\",[])\n        rule=c.get(\"rule\",\"\")+\"/\"+(c.get(\"rulePayload\") or \"\")\n        marker=\"✓\" if ch and ch[-1]==\"AI\" else \"✗\"\n        print(f\"  {marker} {h:45s} rule={rule:30s} chain={\\\" → \\\".join(ch)}\")\nPY' 2>&1",
  "description": "Final verification of all AI domains"
}
```

## soar 改造完成总结

| 项 | 改造前 | 改造后 |
|---|---|---|
| 订阅 | 无（写死 `US-家宽` webshare） | **WestData 完整订阅**（providers/WestData.yaml inline 进 config）|
| AI 代理 | `[REDACTED]:6539` webshare residential | `us17.kookeey.info:24754` ISP Proxy 直连 |
| AI group | `🤖 AI专线` (含 4 个测试 IP 域名) | `AI` selector → kookeey-direct, DIRECT |
| AI 域名规则 | 14 条（claude/openai/gemini 基础）| 32 条（覆盖 anthropic / claude / openai / chatgpt / oai* / sora / arkose / livekit / xai / grok / gemini / deepseek + Anthropic 自家 sentry/datadog）|
| 兜底 | MATCH,DIRECT | 用 WestData 完整 4263 条规则（YouTube/Telegram/Google/Apple 等按 sub 原配走）|
| 出口 IP | [REDACTED] BOS | **[REDACTED] LAX** ✓ 与 Mac/iPhone 一致 |
| AI 域名延迟 | — | ~1.0s（HEAD 包括 TLS 握手）|

## 与 Mac/iPhone 的关键区别

- **Mac/iPhone**：经 trojan 出墙（CN home → JP/HK → kookeey LA），所以用 chain `kookeey-via-HK01` (dialer-proxy)
- **soar**：本身宿主机出口已在境外（[REDACTED] US ColoCrossing），**直连 kookeey 即可**，不需要 chain。少一段中转，延迟更低 + 更稳

## 备份

- `/etc/mihomo/config.yaml.webshare-bak` (旧 config)
- `/etc/mihomo/providers/WestData.yaml.bak`（如果之前有）

## cron 同步行为

soar 没有 cron 自动同步（与路由器/Mac 不同）。如果将来 WestData 订阅有更新或 kookeey 凭据轮换，需要手动：
1. scp 新 sub.yaml 到 `/etc/mihomo/providers/WestData.yaml`
2. 跑 `/usr/local/bin/mihomo-update-config`
3. `curl -X PUT -d '{"path":"/etc/mihomo/config.yaml"}' http://127.0.0.1:9090/configs`

soar 上的客户端工具（不管是用 socks5://127.0.0.1:7891 还是 http://127.0.0.1:7890）连 AI 服务时现在都会从 LA [REDACTED] 出去，跟 Mac/iPhone 完全一致 —— anthropic/openai 看到的 client IP 三端统一，不会触发跨地域反滥用风控。

数据完整了。最终结论：

## soar 真实形态

| 项 | 数据 |
|---|---|
| SSH 入口 | 阿里云 [REDACTED]:6000（北京区段）|
| 部署形态 | **Docker 容器**（eth0 = 172.17.0.5 docker bridge）|
| 出口 IP | **[REDACTED]**，注册 LA / El Segundo / AS63981 NTDKL |
| **真实物理位置** | **疑似亚太**（RTT 156ms 到 LA Cloudflare/kookeey —— 标准跨太平洋延迟） |
| 出海机制 | 推测通过 GRE/IPIP/wireguard 隧道连接美国 NAT 出口 |

**结论：宿主机出口在境外但容器物理疑似亚太**。这意味着 soar 直连 AI 已经能用美国 IP（[REDACTED]），但 ASN 是小厂 NTDKL，可能被 AI 风控当 datacenter。

## 延迟（HEAD `/`，TLS 握手到结束）

| 目标 | 直连 | 经 kookeey | 差额 |
|---|---|---|---|
| api.anthropic.com | 634ms | 949ms | +315ms |
| claude.ai | 641ms | 952ms | +311ms |
| api.openai.com | 636ms | 945ms | +309ms |
| chatgpt.com | 488ms | 1105ms | +617ms |

**经 kookeey 平均多 ~310ms**（多一段跨太平洋 RTT 到 kookeey LA）。

## 带宽（cloudflare speed test）

| 方向 | 直连 | 经 kookeey | 损失 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **下行 50MB** | 88 Mbps（4.6s）| 11.6 Mbps（34.4s）| -87% |
| **上行 10MB** | 27.8 Mbps（2.9s）| 4.2 Mbps（19s）| -85% |

对 AI 用例的实际影响：

| 场景 | 上传量 | 经 kookeey 耗时 |
|---|---|---|
| 普通 API 请求 | 50 KB | 95ms |
| 大 context (200K token) | 600 KB | 1.1s |
| 极端 1M token Opus | 3 MB | 5.7s |

## 决策建议

**两种选择，看你的优先级：**

**A. 现状（AI 走 kookeey）**：保证三端（Mac/iPhone/soar）出口 IP 一致（[REDACTED]），避免 anthropic 跨地域风控。代价：延迟 +310ms，带宽 -85%。**适合：每条 API 请求都关心 IP 一致性（账号风控敏感、跨设备无缝切换）。**

**B. soar 直连**：删除 AI rules 让 AI 直接走 sub Final/DIRECT，soar 出口 [REDACTED]。延迟低 60%，带宽高 5-8 倍。代价：出口 IP 与 Mac/iPhone 不同（38.71.x vs 172.96.x），AS63981 小厂 ASN 可能被 anthropic 标记。**适合：soar 上跑 batch 任务、大 context、流式吞吐场景。**

要不要切到 B 方案？或者**做个混合**：保留两个 group（`AI` = kookeey 用于敏感账号请求，`AI-Fast` = DIRECT 用于批量吞吐），让你的脚本根据场景选？

**找到关键线索 —— `frpc`**（Fast Reverse Proxy 客户端）！这就解释了为什么 SSH 入口在阿里云但出口在美国：

```
入口路径: SSH client → 阿里云 [REDACTED]:6000 (frps) → frp 隧道 → soar 容器内 sshd
出口路径: soar 容器 → docker bridge → docker host → ISP 出口 [REDACTED]
入口和出口走的是完全不同的两条路！
```

## 最终调查结论

### 真实形态全貌

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  SSH 入口路径 (反向代理)                                       │
│  你 → 阿里云 frps [REDACTED]:6000 (北京)                  │
│     ↓ frp 隧道                                                │
│  soar 容器内 sshd:22                                          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  出站路径 (完全独立于入口)                                     │
│  soar 容器 eth0 (172.17.0.5)                                 │
│     ↓ docker bridge                                          │
│  docker host (aistack 集群节点)                              │
│     ↓ NAT pool 借美国 IP block                                │
│  公网出口: [REDACTED] (LA / AS63981 NTDKL)                  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 关键证据

| 证据 | 来源 |
|---|---|
| **Docker 容器** | `/.dockerenv` exists, `systemd-detect-virt = docker` |
| **frpc 主动连阿里云做反代** | `/etc/frp/frpc.toml`: serverAddr=[REDACTED]:7000，暴露 SSH+mosh |
| **GPU AI 推理服务器** | 跑着 SGLang + MiniCPM-SALA + jupyter-lab；`/etc/hosts` 有 `head.aistack.local` |
| **多容器集群环境** | `head.aistack.local` (172.31.0.41) 暗示 K8s/Slurm 类调度器 |
| **出口 IP 注册 LA 但物理疑非美国** | [REDACTED] = AS63981 NTDKL；到 CF/Google anycast RTT **130ms**（真在 LA 应 < 30ms） |
| **到中国 IP 静默 drop** | 百度/阿里/字节/HK 全 0s timeout —— aistack 故意屏蔽国内回流路径 |
| **容器内无任何代理/隧道/iptables** | 路由极简，路由表只有默认网关 + docker bridge；nftables 空；无 wg/tun/gre 网卡 |
| **容器时区 CST (UTC+8)** | 但容器时区可任意设，不能据此判定物理位置 |

### "未检出的代理 / 乱七八糟的"调查结论

**容器内没有任何隐藏代理**：
- iptables/nftables 空
- 没有 LD_PRELOAD
- 没有 ProxyChains
- 没有 wireguard / tun / gre 隧道虚拟网卡
- DNS 走 8.8.8.8（无 cn-DNS 污染）
- 路由表只有 docker bridge 默认网关

**但 docker host 上必然有"出海"机制**（你看不到，docker 容器视角永远看不到 host），最可能的形态：
1. host 上做 SNAT 把出站 NAT 到一个境外 NAT 节点
2. 或者 host 自己跑 wireguard，把 docker bridge 的流量隧道到境外
3. 同时**故意 black-hole 国内 IP**（避免业务回流国内被 GFW 二次过滤）

### 物理位置最可能的判断

**不在 LA**（130ms RTT 排除），**不在中国大陆**（国内 IP 不通），**130ms 到美西 anycast 符合东亚 → 美西海缆**：
- 最可能：**HK 或 JP 的 GPU datacenter**，出站经过 aistack 的境外 NAT pool（借 LA 美国 IP）
- 也可能：**SG datacenter**（130-170ms 到美西）

### 对你实际使用的影响

物理位置不确定**不影响功能**：
- AI 流量经 kookeey 出 [REDACTED] LA → 与 Mac/iPhone 一致 ✓
- 直连备用路径在 [REDACTED] LA（出口 IP 是 NAT pool）✓
- frp 入口稳定可达（虽然 SSH session 频繁断，可能阿里云 frps 配置了 idle timeout 或 sshd 上有 ClientAliveCountMax 限制）

### 顺便的优化建议（与代理无关）

ssh 频繁断开 "Connection closed by [REDACTED]" 是 frps 那一端的限制。如果想稳，在 `~/.ssh/config` 的 `Host soar` 下加：
```
ServerAliveInterval 30
ServerAliveCountMax 3
TCPKeepAlive yes
```
这会让你这边定期发 keepalive，避免 frps 看你 idle 主动 close。
